    2190             : 
    2191             : /**
    2192             :  * Mapping to find suitable missing linked liboverrides to replace by the newly generated linked
    2193             :  * liboverrides during resync process.
    2194             :  *
    2195             :  * \note About Order:
    2196             :  * In most cases, if there are several virtual linked liboverrides generated with the same base
    2197             :  * name (like `OBCube.001`, `OBCube.002`, etc.), this mapping system will find the correct one, for
    2198             :  * the following reasons:
    2199             :  *  - Order of creation of these virtual IDs in resync process is expected to be stable (i.e.
    2200             :  * several runs of resync code based on the same linked data would re-create the same virtual
    2201             :  * liboverride IDs in the same order);
    2202             :  *  - Order of creation and usage of the mapping data (a FIFO queue) also ensures that the missing
    2203             :  * placeholder `OBCube.001` is always 're-used' before `OBCube.002`.
    2204             :  *
    2205             :  * In case linked data keep being modified, these conditions may fail and the mapping may start to
    2206             :  * return 'wrong' results. However, this is considered as an acceptable limitation here, since this
    2207             :  * is mainly a 'best effort' to recover from situations that should not be happening in the first
    2208             :  * place.
    2209             :  */
    2210             : 
    2211             : using LibOverrideMissingIDsData_Key = const std::pair<std::string, Library *>;
    2212             : using LibOverrideMissingIDsData_Value = std::deque<ID *>;
    2213             : using LibOverrideMissingIDsData =
    2214             :     std::map<LibOverrideMissingIDsData_Key, LibOverrideMissingIDsData_Value>;
    2215             : 
    2216             : /* Return a key suitable for the missing IDs mapping, i.e. a pair of
    2217             :  * `<full ID name (including first two ID type chars) without a potential numeric extension,
    2218             :  *   ID library>`.
    2219             :  *
    2220             :  * So e.g. returns `<"OBMyObject", lib>` for ID from `lib` with names like `"OBMyObject"`,
    2221             :  * `"OBMyObject.002"`, `"OBMyObject.12345"`, and so on, but _not_ `"OBMyObject.12.002"`.
    2222             :  */
    2223           2 : static LibOverrideMissingIDsData_Key lib_override_library_resync_missing_id_key(ID *id)
